HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_116cfe993a166d28a9c7","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:21:1\",\"root_000516\",[\"B002\"]],[\"88:21:4\",\"root_000516\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Hatırlatmanın sert ve keskin olma ihtimali hemen sonraki denetim reddiyle düzeltilir: Söz açık ve sarsıcı olabilir, fakat kişilere uygulanan güce dönüşemez.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B008","root_000516/B009"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_132c00189e4b8954a4fb","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:21:1\",\"root_000516\",[\"B008\",\"B009\"]],[\"88:21:4\",\"root_000516\",[\"B008\",\"B009\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Hatırlatmanın hak belgesini de çağrıştırması, onu dönüş ve hesaptan önce verilmiş sorumluluk doğuran bir bildirim gibi gösterir; bildirimi yapan, sonraki işlemleri yönetmez.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_25c29b39dcb164158b87","text":"3. **The forceful branch is activated only to be corrected.** **Lexical:** `ذ ك ر` B002, \"hard, sharp, forceful\" imagery, beside B009. **Cue:** the imperative can sound forceful, but `لَّسْتَ عَلَيْهِم بِمُصَيْطِرٍ` in 88:22 explicitly removes coercive oversight. **Role:** B002 supplies the dormant possibility of hard imposition; the following negation blocks that role while retaining verbal sharpness or clarity. **Abduction:** I infer a deliberate correction from immediate sequence. **Reading change:** the reminder may be incisive, but it cannot become force exercised over persons.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2f1775ad99c16bad03d8","text":"3. **Legal document resonance before account.** Evidence: `ذ ك ر` B008 rights document/legal instrument; later `ح س ب` B001 reckoning; `ع م ل` B003 official charge and `س ع ي` B002 endeavor. Structural cue: reminder stands between signs and final return/account. Role: the reminder functions like notice before accounting. Abductive move: accountability requires prior notification preserved in memory or record. Reading change: the ayah gains legal-administrative force without becoming coercion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3bd24039af2df968eaf8","text":"88:22 immediately corrected the forceful `ذ ك ر` B002 possibility by denying control. 88:25-26 then revealed the reminder's procedural limit: it prepares attention, while return and account belong to a later operation explicitly claimed by the divine side.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_637fa20e6cb365e7103d","text":"1. **Reminder as bringing to mind after visual signs.** Evidence: `ذ ك ر` B003 bringing to mind, B009 reminder/admonition; preceding `ن ظ ر` B001 looking and the four created signs. Structural cue: the command follows the inspection sequence. Role: looking supplies material; reminder activates retention and recognition. Abductive move: perception alone needs verbal recollection to become accountable understanding. Reading change: the ayah reads as converting observed order into remembered obligation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B004","root_000516/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_6a6dc18af0f709f9309f","text":"2. **The repeated root defines a bounded communicative office.** **Lexical:** `ذ ك ر` B004, \"mention on the tongue,\" and B009; the construction is restricted by `إِنَّمَا`. **Cue:** `ذَكِّرْ ... مُذَكِّرٌ` encloses the second-person pronoun, and 88:22 immediately negates control. **Role:** B004 supplies verbal articulation, B009 supplies its admonitory purpose, and restriction prevents speech from expanding into domination. **Abduction:** I infer that root repetition functions as a job description. **Reading change:** the ayah says not merely \"give a reminder\" but \"remain within reminding as your defined mode of action.\"","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a5a6b2cf16348760d079","text":"2. **Mention without control.** Evidence: `ذ ك ر` B004 mention on the tongue, B007 honorable mention; next `س طر` B003 control is negated. Structural cue: command to remind is immediately bounded by “not controller.” Role: the messenger’s task is utterance/admonition, not domination. Abductive move: truth can be made present without coercive possession of hearers. Reading change: `مذكر` becomes a limited speech office.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B003","root_000516/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_cc3ebb8d4fef2b6a523a","text":"1. **Reminder converts distributed visible evidence into recalled understanding.** **Lexical:** `ذ ك ر` B003, \"bringing a thing to mind,\" and B009, \"reminder or admonition.\" **Cue:** causal/sequential `فَـ` follows the four looking questions of 88:17-20; the same root appears first as imperative and then as agent noun. **Role:** the imperative initiates cognitive return, and the agent noun defines the speaker by that repeated operation. **Abduction:** I infer that the observed camel, sky, mountains, and earth are the reminder's immediate evidentiary content. **Reading change:** reminding is not insertion of alien information but re-presenting what visible construction should already make available to mind.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B003","root_000516/B009"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d81ff655b896b661b9c5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:21:1\",\"root_000516\",[\"B003\",\"B009\"]],[\"88:21:4\",\"root_000516\",[\"B003\",\"B009\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Hatırlatma, deve, gök, dağ ve yerde dağıtılmış görünür kanıtları insanın zihninde yeniden hazır bulunan bir anlayışa dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e0907bb0851ba08cd0eb","text":"4. **Reminder prepares for return and account without administering either.** **Lexical:** `ذ ك ر` B003/B009; later `ء و ب` B001 and `ح س ب` B001 name return and reckoning in 88:25-26. **Cue:** the role restriction is followed by turning away, punishment, return, and account. **Role:** reminder restores the matter to attention before a process whose control belongs elsewhere. **Abduction:** I infer preparatory function from order, not that reminder causes judgment. **Reading change:** the ayah occupies a precise procedural place: cognitive notice before divine disposition, with no transfer of jurisdiction.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_f3e7106aef17225e9b76","text":"The immediate `لست عليهم بمصيطر` forced a narrow reading of the command: it is intense but bounded. The final `حسابهم` made the reminder feel like accountable notice, a record-trigger rather than force.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B004","root_000516/B009"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f9633d573a13271b1426","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:21:1\",\"root_000516\",[\"B004\",\"B009\"]],[\"88:21:4\",\"root_000516\",[\"B004\",\"B009\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Hatırlatma sözünün emir ve görev adı olarak tekrarı, konuşmayı kişinin sınırlandırılmış işi yapar; sözlü bildirim insanlara hükmetmeye genişleyemez.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
